Nuclear Fission completes in 1 turn with 14 uranium ready. Maximize UseNuke (100) for immediate atomic deployment. Maintain Defense/CityDefense/Mobilization at 100 to survive until deterrent operational. Reduce Naval from 95 to 85 as American destroyer threat is severe but manageable. Boost Espionage to 100 to protect science cities and maintain election rigging in Enns. Increase Spaceship to 95 as backup victory path if culture is disrupted. Diplomacy raised to 80 to counter Greece's 34 delegates in upcoming World Congress vote on United Nations proposal.

- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Catherine, leader of Russia (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Russia
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Cossack, Replacing Cavalry (Requires technology: Military Science)
        - Unique Unit: Licorne, Replacing Field Gun (Requires technology: Rifling)
        - Unique Building: Ostrog, Replacing Bastion Fort (Requires technology: Navigation)
        - Unique Building: Pogost, Replacing Customs House (Requires technology: Guilds)
        - Civilization Ability: All Strategic Resource deposits provide double their normal quantity. Receive 20 Science when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Culture cost of acquiring new tiles reduced by 33%.
      - Leader: Catherine
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Nuclear Fission: Allows you to build the Bazooka, the most powerful ranged Unit and Atomic Bombs, the first nuclear weapon in the game (note: you must first have completed the Manhattan Project and have Uranium available). Also allows you to build the Nuclear Plant, a building which requires Uranium and increases the Production of a City.
        Completing it would unlock: Satellites, Advanced Ballistics
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      
      ## Policies
      - Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Dictatorship of the Proletariat: +7 Food, Science, Gold, and Culture per city.
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Statecraft (New Branch):
        - Statecraft improves your ability to benefit from Trade Routes, City-States, Spies, and the World Congress.
        - Adopting Statecraft grants:
        - * +1 of every Yield in Capital for every 20 Citizens in your Empire.
        - * +1 Gold in every City.
        - Each Statecraft policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Gold in every City, and +10% Influence from Diplomatic Missions.
        - Adopting all policies in Statecraft grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Palace of Westminster.
        - * Every World Congress Session, gain 5 Culture, Science, and Gold for each Delegate you control, scaling with Era.
        - * World Wonders require 1 less unlocked Policy for every 3 City-State alliances you maintain.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Diplomats with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 8
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 2
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 7
        - Boldness: 6
        - WarBias: 6
        - HostileBias: 5
        - WarmongerHate: 8
        - NeutralBias: 3
        - FriendlyBias: 6
        - GuardedBias: 10
        - AfraidBias: 5
        - DiplomaticBalance: 3
        - Friendliness: 8
        - WorkWithWillingness: 8
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 5
        - Loyalty: 9
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 7
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 5
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 4
        - MinorCivWarBias: 4
        - DenounceWillingness: 5
        - Forgiveness: 10
        - Meanness: 4
        - Neediness: 7
        - Chattiness: 10
        - DeceptiveBias: 2
        - Rationale: Escalating to total defensive mobilization with WarBias increased from 5 to 6 signals that only military resistance can ensure survival against Greece's 3:1 superiority. Raising GuardedBias to 10 (maximum) and HostileBias to 5 reflects existential threat posture. Reducing FriendlyBias to 6 acknowledges wartime reality while maintaining diplomatic channels with Order bloc allies.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Nuclear Fission
        - Rationale: Nuclear Fission remains the absolute survival priority. With Rocketry completing in 3 turns and 14 uranium stockpiled, atomic bombs provide the only credible deterrent against Greece's overwhelming military advantage. The +15% production from nuclear plants also accelerates cultural infrastructure and spaceship components for alternative victory paths.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: People's Army (Policy)
        - Rationale: Maintaining People's Army provides crucial dual benefits: +5 culture per public school accelerates tourism victory (4 turns to Influential with America, 36 with Greece) while +2 happiness maintains wartime stability across 12 cities. The +100% Military Academy production with 5 free academies directly counters Greek military pressure and synergizes with defensive mobilization.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Bratislava
        - Public: 65
        - Private: 65
        - Rationale: America's heavy attacks on Bratislava (city down to 14 HP) directly threaten defensive pact ally England's ally. Increase support from 50/50 to 65/65 to signal stronger diplomatic backing without provoking direct war. This maintains plausible deniability while showing America their aggression has consequences. My spy Yulia is already rigging elections there; this diplomatic boost may help Bratislava survive and preserve WC vote distribution.
        - UpdatedTurn: 294
        
        ### India
        - Public: 75
        - Private: 75
        - Rationale: Strengthen India relationship from 65/65 to 75/75 to secure their 2 WC votes for upcoming diplomatic victory attempt in 14 turns. India is isolated and militarily weak (85 strength), making them highly receptive to diplomatic overtures. This investment strengthens our voting bloc to 33 combined delegates, providing additional buffer against potential Greek defection or city-state losses. High values also encourage continued India cooperation post-WC for cultural victory open borders.
        - UpdatedTurn: 308
        
        ### Abernethy
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Reduce Abernethy relations from 95/95 to 0/0 to stop wasting diplomatic capital on failed city-state flip attempts. Previous maximum investment failed to overcome Greek sphere of influence and 635 influence advantage. Resources better spent on France peace and defensive preparations. Greek control over this city-state is locked until we can contest it militarily.
        - UpdatedTurn: 346
        
        ### France
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: Strengthening France from 80/80 to 90/90 secures their 5 WC votes for Decolonization proposal today and positions for future diplomatic cooperation. France shares Order ideology, just made peace, and is militarily weak (3734 strength), making them highly receptive. With Greece at 30 votes, I need every delegate to block their diplomatic victory. Post-vote, high values will enable open borders for tourism flow.
        - UpdatedTurn: 372
        
        ### Poland
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Maximize Poland from 100/95 to 100/100 for their 5 WC votes. Poland shares Order ideology and is at war with America - their votes are critical to block Greece's diplomatic victory. Post-war cooperation also needed for science/cultural victory paths. High values ensure continued open borders for tourism flow.
        - UpdatedTurn: 383
        
        ### Greece
        - Public: -50
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: Escalate Greek hostility from -25/-25 to -50/-50 to signal maximum defensive posture. Their 3:1 military advantage, surprise war declaration, and imminent diplomatic victory require zero-compromise stance. Low values reduce peace willingness (pointless) and signal to allies that total war mobilization is necessary for survival.
        - UpdatedTurn: 383
        
        ### Babylon
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Maximizing Babylon relations from 95 to 100 secures their critical 8 World Congress votes against Greece's diplomatic victory proposal (United Nations). Babylon is our defensive pact ally and shares our Order ideology - their votes are essential to block Greek proposals and pass our Decolonization initiative in the upcoming 8-turn vote. This also counters their unhappiness and maintains loyalty during existential crisis.
        - UpdatedTurn: 388
        
        ### England
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: Increasing England from 90 to 95 reinforces loyalty with our vassal who controls 9 World Congress votes. England is militarily vulnerable (strength 849) and geographically positioned as our buffer against Greek expansion. High relations ensure their continued delegation support and prevent vassal unrest during simultaneous wars with Greece and America, while securing open borders for tourism flow to America.
        - UpdatedTurn: 388
        
        ### America
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: Increasing America relations from 80/80 to 90/90 encourages peace negotiations once nuclear deterrence is established. This acknowledges their naval aggression while maintaining sufficiently high values for future cooperation, allowing us to focus on the greater Greek threat. Post-atomic bomb deployment, these values will facilitate peace and open borders for tourism.
        - UpdatedTurn: 388
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Increasing UseNuke to 80 prepares for imminent atomic bomb deployment (3 turns until Nuclear Fission). Boosting Naval to 95 and NavalRecon to 80 counters severe American naval threat in western seas. Maintaining Defense/CityDefense/Mobilization at 100 ensures survival against Greece's 3:1 land advantage. Espionage remains at 100 to maintain intelligence advantage. Cultural victory remains primary path while building nuclear deterrent for survival.
        - GrandStrategy: Culture
        
        ### Flavors
        - Offense: 30
        - Defense: 100
        - Mobilization: 100
        - CityDefense: 100
        - MilitaryTraining: 60
        - Recon: 35
        - Ranged: 90

            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: 384
          - Greek Sphere of Influence: Abernethy:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: 373
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Greek Sphere of Influence: La Venta:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 373
          - Natural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each Natural Wonder provides +2 to all Yields when worked.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Open Door: Bornu:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Religion: Buddhism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: United Nations:
            - Description: Begins the United Nations project. Once underway, Civilizations can contribute Production towards its completion by selecting it in the city production list. When complete, Civilizations receive bonuses based on how much they contributed.
              : United Nations wonder appears in Capital. (Receive 1 Additional Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World. Also, when you expend a Great Person, earn 25 Influence with all City-States you have met. Triggers a Golden Age, as well as the United Nations Special Session of the World Congress if the world has reached the Atomic Era.)
              : Receive an immediate boost of 25 Influence with all City-States. The Consulate National Wonder appears in Capital. (Receive 1 Additional Delegate in the World Congress for every 8 City-States originally in the World.)
              : Receive a free Great Diplomat.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - Proposer: Greece
          - Repeal: Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - Proposer: England
          - Enact: Decolonization:
            - Description: Enforce global decolonization. If successful, the Influence of all Players with all City-States is lowered to 50, unless they are permanent allies.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Radio.
            - Proposer: Russia

        You, Catherine (leader of Russia, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 389.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Catherine, leader of Russia (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Russia
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Cossack, Replacing Cavalry (Requires technology: Military Science)
        - Unique Unit: Licorne, Replacing Field Gun (Requires technology: Rifling)
        - Unique Building: Ostrog, Replacing Bastion Fort (Requires technology: Navigation)
        - Unique Building: Pogost, Replacing Customs House (Requires technology: Guilds)
        - Civilization Ability: All Strategic Resource deposits provide double their normal quantity. Receive 20 Science when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Culture cost of acquiring new tiles reduced by 33%.
      - Leader: Catherine
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Electronics: Allows you to build the Police Station, increasing City security level.
        Completing it would unlock: Alternative Energy, Telecommunications
      - Nuclear Fission: Allows you to build the Bazooka, the most powerful ranged Unit and Atomic Bombs, the first nuclear weapon in the world (note: you must first have completed the Manhattan Project and have Uranium available). Also allows you to build the Nuclear Plant, a building which requires Uranium and increases the Production of a City.
        Completing it would unlock: Satellites, Advanced Ballistics
      - Combined Arms: Allows you to build the Tank, a fast and deadly armor Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Radar
      
      ## Policies
      - Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Dictatorship of the Proletariat: +7 Food, Science, Gold, and Culture per city.
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Statecraft (New Branch):
        - Statecraft improves your ability to benefit from Trade Routes, City-States, Spies, and the World Congress.
        - Adopting Statecraft grants:
        - * +1 of every Yield in Capital for every 20 Citizens in your Empire.
        - * +1 Gold in every City.
        - Each Statecraft policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Gold in every City, and +10% Influence from Diplomatic Missions.
        - Adopting all policies in Statecraft grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Palace of Westminster.
        - * Every World Congress Session, gain 5 Culture, Science, and Gold for each Delegate you control, scaling with Era.
        - * World Wonders require 1 less unlocked Policy for every 3 City-State alliances you maintain.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Diplomats with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Nuclear Fission
        - Rationale: Nuclear Fission is our absolute defense and diplomatic pivot. With Rocketry completed, starting Nuclear Fission will allow us to build Atomic Bombs. This will provide a massive military power rating boost, triggering nuclear deterrence that will make America and Greece highly receptive to peace offers. Peace will then allow us to establish Open Borders and secure our Cultural Victory.
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Policy: People's Army
        - Rationale: People's Army (Order Level 1 Tenet) is the optimal next policy choice. It will provide 5 free Military Academies to accelerate defensive unit production, and more importantly, +5 Culture and +2 Happiness per Public School across our 12 cities. This massive injection of culture and happiness (+60 culture, +24 happiness base) will dramatically accelerate our progress toward future policy tenets (like Cultural Revolution), protect us from enemy tourism, and raise our happiness above Greece's to prepare for future tourism modifiers.
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Maximize relations with our voluntary vassal England from 95 to 100. Elizabeth is currently unhappy about her lost territories to Greece and America ("failed to protect territory"). Raising relations to the maximum (100/100) will guarantee her total diplomatic loyalty, ensuring her 9 critical World Congress delegates vote with our bloc to pass Decolonization and defeat Greece's United Nations proposal in 6 turns. This also secures long-term post-war cooperation.
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - TargetID: 7
    - #3:
      - toolName: keep-status-quo
      - rationale: Our current strategy and flavors are perfectly tuned for our dual goals of defense and cultural victory. Maintaining 100 Defense, 100 Mobilization, 100 CityDefense, and 100 Air/Antiair ensures our survival against Greece's land army and America's naval forces. Meanwhile, keeping 100 Espionage preserves our intel network inside Greek cities, and keeping Culture at 80 and Tourism/Religion high will carry us to victory in 4 turns for America and 35 turns for Greece.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Deciding Russia's Path**
            
            I'm currently evaluating Russia's position after turn 389, with a focus on the unlocked Science Victory in the Information Age. My primary thought is to strategize Catherine's next moves to capitalize on this.
            
            **Securing Cultural Dominance**
            
            My focus has shifted back to securing a Cultural Victory, as we're on the cusp of achieving it. With America nearing influential status and Greece strategically vulnerable due to their current war with us, I'm prioritizing actions that will accelerate our tourism output and potentially cripple Greece's ability to counter. The upcoming UN vote on Decolonization presents a critical opportunity to disrupt their CS alliances and solidify our path to victory.
            
            **Analyzing UN Vote Impact**
            
            I'm deeply engaged in the upcoming UN vote, strategizing to leverage our 35 votes against Greece's 34 to push through Decolonization. This move is crucial to disrupt their influence over non-permanent city-states, thereby reducing their delegate count and securing our path to a Cultural Victory.
            
            **Assessing Global Landscape**
            
            I'm re-evaluating the remaining players and their allegiances following France and India's defeat. America's extensive conflicts and Greece's current wars are significant factors. I am now focusing on identifying the most advantageous diplomatic and military positions given this updated geopolitical map.
            
            **Updating Research Priority**
            
            My research focus has shifted to Nuclear Fission, now that Rocketry is completing this turn. This is critical for atomic bomb development and maintaining deterrence. I'm double-checking if this remains our optimal research path.
            
            **Evaluating Policy Options**
            
            My current focus is on selecting the most impactful social policy for the next five turns. Given the recent acquisition of five free Military Academies and the boost to public schools, I'm re-examining the benefits of policies that synergize with this infrastructure, particularly those enhancing culture and happiness.
            
            **Revising Policy Choices**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our social policies, specifically looking at Order Level 2 tenets. While Cultural Revolution offers a compelling tourism boost against less happy civs, I'm questioning if Five-Year Plan's production focus is ultimately more advantageous given our current victory conditions.
            
            **Selecting Social Policy**
            
            I am prioritizing the selection of social policies, specifically deliberating between Level 1 and Level 2 tenets. The acquisition of five free Military Academies and boosted public schools necessitates a re-evaluation. My current focus is on People's Army, but I'm weighing its benefits against other options like Dictatorship of the Proletariat.
            
            **Defending Coastal Territories**
            
            I'm reviewing recent naval engagements, noting the loss of a dreadnought to America. My current focus is on assessing the combat near Coast 8,28, specifically the outcome where America's destroyer was damaged. I'm verifying the details of this engagement to understand our defensive posture.
            
            **Assessing Naval Engagements**
            
            I'm closely examining recent naval combat reports, particularly the destruction of two enemy destroyers and the successful defense of our coastal territories. My current focus is on analyzing the significant American naval presence near Marseille and evaluating our fleet's readiness to counter this threat, while also noting our recently completed naval units.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Strategy**
            
            I am currently analyzing the implications of the active resolutions, particularly "Russian Sphere of Influence: Enns." My primary concern is preventing Greece from winning the UN project, which would grant them a diplomatic victory. I'm evaluating how enacting Decolonization will impact their City-State alliances and delegate count.
            
            **Investigating Resolution Mechanics**
            
            I'm trying to understand how "EnactedOn: -1" applies to active resolutions like "Passport System" and Greek/Russian spheres of influence. It seems to indicate they are indeed active, possibly from an earlier mechanism or even from game start. My focus is now on confirming this and how it impacts current City-State allegiances, especially regarding Greece's control over Sofia, Utica, and Huari.
            
            **Confirming Greece's Influence**
            
            I'm verifying Greece's overwhelming control over city-states, confirming they are the major ally to ten out of eleven, directly contributing to their delegate count. This reinforces the critical need to disrupt their alliances.
            
            **Disrupting Greek Influence**
            
            Decolonization has passed, which has drastically reduced Greece's influence with most of their city-states. This is a significant win, as they've lost their major alliances with Utica, Huari, Sidon, Edinburgh, Jerusalem, Kyzyl, and Wittenberg, severely impacting their delegate count and our path to victory.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm confirming our successful vote on Decolonization, which has critically diminished Greece's delegate count from 34 to an estimated 13-17. This pivotal moment, with our bloc of 35 votes overcoming Greece's 34, effectively prevents their diplomatic victory and solidifies our own path forward.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Maneuvers**
            
            I'm reassessing our relationships with other nations in light of our ongoing war with America. Despite our current conflict, our high public and private opinion scores suggest a disconnect with the actual diplomatic state, which warrants further investigation and adjustment to align with our strategic goals.
            
            **Developing Deterrence Capabilities**
            
            My immediate focus is on leveraging our robust science output to research Nuclear Fission swiftly. The plan is to then build Atomic Bombs using our available Uranium, establishing crucial nuclear deterrence. This will pave the way for peace with America, enabling essential Open Borders for tourism and advancing our victory condition.
            
            **Analyzing Cultural Levers**
            
            I am currently analyzing how religious differences and existing tourism modifiers, such as the already enacted Passport System, interact with potential Open Borders agreements. I'm specifically investigating the impact of our Buddhism on Greece's cities and whether this, combined with impending influence over America, can accelerate our cultural victory.
            
            **Accelerating Tourism to Greece**
            
            I'm now focused on accelerating my tourism output to influence Greece. Peace with America is a prerequisite for Open Borders, which would grant a significant tourism modifier, potentially speeding up our Cultural Victory by over 30 turns.
            
            **Analyzing Tourism Modifiers**
            
            I'm investigating how existing policies and the Passport System interact with Open Borders to maximize tourism to Greece. Establishing trade routes and leveraging our Order ideology are key considerations, while the status of Buddhism as a World Religion is also under scrutiny to optimize our cultural victory path.
            
            **Confirming World Religion**
            
            I've confirmed Buddhism is our active World Religion, granting a substantial tourism bonus to Moscow. This is a significant advantage for accelerating my cultural victory, especially now that the Passport System is also confirmed as active.
            
            **Researching Nuclear Fission**
            
            I'm confirming our research priority. With Rocketry finishing next turn, I've decided to direct our scientific efforts towards Nuclear Fission. This is the critical step for developing atomic weapons and securing peace, which is essential for our tourism-based victory.
            
            **Selecting People's Army**
            
            My focus is now on social policies. After reviewing the options, I'm proceeding with People's Army. While Cultural Revolution offers tourism benefits, the production focus of People's Army is more advantageous for our current victory conditions.
            
            **Evaluating Policy Trade-offs**
            
            I'm weighing the immediate happiness and culture gains from People's Army against the potential tourism surge from Cultural Revolution if Greece's happiness dips. The latter hinges on precise timing and Greek fortunes, while People's Army offers a guaranteed significant boost to my culture and happiness.
            
            **Choosing People's Army**
            
            I've decided to select People's Army. The increased happiness and culture it provides, along with production boosts for military academies, aligns perfectly with my goals of securing a Cultural Victory while bolstering defenses against Greece. This policy offers a guaranteed advantage.
            
            **Reviewing Unit Strengths**
            
            I'm reassessing my current unit strengths based on available "flavors." My defensive, mobilization, and city defense are all maxed out. Ranged, Nuke, and Naval capabilities are also very high, which is crucial given current conflicts. My Air and Anti-Air are also at 100.
            
            **Prioritizing Trade Routes**
            
            I'm shifting focus to utilizing my three available trade routes. My cities' production queues are being re-evaluated, particularly Moscow and Rostov, to optimize the deployment of caravans and cargo ships for maximum economic benefit while continuing to research Nuclear Fission.
            
            **Assigning Trade Routes**
            
            I'm confirming the automatic assignment of our three trade routes based on our current flavors, maximizing religious spread and gold generation. My spy operations continue in Greece, focusing on gathering intelligence in various city-states, with Agent Igor and Doktor Seriy making significant progress in their respective networks.
            
            **Gathering Intelligence**
            
            My spies are deeply embedded within Greece, providing exceptional visibility into their cities and plans. I'm focusing on maintaining our espionage flavor at 100% to ensure continued intel gathering and technological disruption. Our relationships with most civs are excellent, though Greece remains hostile after their defeat.
            
            **Assessing Vassal Mistreatment**
            
            I'm investigating England's perception of mistreatment as my vassal, specifically her claim that I failed to protect her territory. This is particularly perplexing as she recently lost London, and I'm trying to reconcile this with my understanding of our diplomatic and military status.
            
            **Strengthening Vassal Loyalty**
            
            I'm ensuring England's continued loyalty. Despite her territorial losses and current wars, our exceptionally high relationship modifier should suffice. I'm also confirming Babylon's loyalty remains at its maximum, as their votes are vital.
            
            **Adjusting Diplomatic Postures**
            
            I'm finalizing diplomatic relationships, ensuring Poland and Babylon remain loyal with maximum votes. America's relationship is set to facilitate swift peace post-deterrence, while Greece remains at maximum hostility. England's loyalty is also being reinforced. I'm initiating research and policy updates for Nuclear Fission and People's Army, respectively, to maintain our current strategic trajectory.
            
            **Adjusting Flavor Settings**
            
            I'm reassessing our current flavor settings. While they appear strong across many categories, I'm double-checking that there aren't subtle adjustments that could be beneficial, particularly in light of diplomatic shifts and upcoming strategic moves. My intent is to maintain these excellent flavors.
            
            **Researching Nuclear Fission**
            
            I'm initiating research into Nuclear Fission, a crucial step for nuclear deterrence and ultimately securing peace with America for Open Borders. This move is vital to accelerate our Cultural Victory.
            
            **Finalizing Policy and Diplomacy**
            
            I'm confirming People's Army as the optimal social policy for happiness and culture. I'm also setting England and Babylon's relationships to maximum to guarantee their votes, ensuring a stable diplomatic bloc for upcoming World Congress votes. America will be positioned for peace post-deterrence.
            
            **Refining Defensive and Cultural Priorities**
            
            My focus is on ensuring our survival with maxed-out defense, air, and anti-air capabilities. Simultaneously, maintaining 100% espionage within Greece is key, as is maximizing our culture and tourism, which are poised to secure victory within a few turns against America and Greece.

        - #2:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: S22k76JnuLNloPVo
          - toolName: set-policy
          - input:
            - Policy: People's Army
            - Rationale: People's Army (Order Level 1 Tenet) is the optimal next policy choice. It will provide 5 free Military Academies to accelerate defensive unit production, and more importantly, +5 Culture and +2 Happiness per Public School across our 12 cities. This massive injection of culture and happiness (+60 culture, +24 happiness base) will dramatically accelerate our progress toward future policy tenets (like Cultural Revolution), protect us from enemy tourism, and raise our happiness above Greece's to prepare for future tourism modifiers.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #3:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: oq2ABPJdf9bLd3s6
          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - Rationale: Maximize relations with our voluntary vassal England from 95 to 100. Elizabeth is currently unhappy about her lost territories to Greece and America ("failed to protect territory"). Raising relations to the maximum (100/100) will guarantee her total diplomatic loyalty, ensuring her 9 critical World Congress delegates vote with our bloc to pass Decolonization and defeat Greece's United Nations proposal in 6 turns. This also secures long-term post-war cooperation.
            - Public: 100
            - Private: 100
            - TargetID: 7
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #4:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: RYhTzug6smRvisLH
          - toolName: keep-status-quo
          - input:
            - Rationale: Our current strategy and flavors are perfectly tuned for our dual goals of defense and cultural victory. Maintaining 100 Defense, 100 Mobilization, 100 CityDefense, and 100 Air/Antiair ensures our survival against Greece's land army and America's naval forces. Meanwhile, keeping 100 Espionage preserves our intel network inside Greek cities, and keeping Culture at 80 and Tourism/Religion high will carry us to victory in 4 turns for America and 35 turns for Greece.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
